    74	## Deferred / Known Issues
    75	
    76	Carried over from prior phases (also listed in
    77	[`anvil/README.md`](anvil/README.md#known-limitations--deferred)):
    78	
    79	1. **SSL cert bind-mount uses an absolute host path** in
    80	   `docker-compose.local.yml` (`/home/dgi/app/DGLab/anvil/docker/nginx/certs`),
    81	   so the rendered `${SSL_CERT}`/`${SSL_KEY}` paths resolve inside nginx.
    82	   Environment-specific; adjust on a different machine.
    83	2. **shellcheck / phpcs / sass not tool-verified in the build environment.** The
    84	   final-phase bash edits are `bash -n` clean and introduced no new shellcheck
    85	   warnings, but `lib/ec2.sh` retains two **pre-existing** shellcheck items from
    86	   Phase 4 (SC2016 info at the availability-zone query; SC2086 warning at the
    87	   route-table query, ~line 146). Left untouched to avoid modifying committed
    88	   Phase 4 logic.
    89	3. **`cloud-init.yaml` repo URL is a placeholder**
    90	   (`https://github.com/example/anvil.git`); replace with the real repo (or bake
    91	   into a custom AMI).
    92	4. **Web UI "stop" also stops its own server** (the server handling the stop
    93	   request terminates itself). Expected behaviour, documented.
    94	5. **RDS provision waits ~20 minutes** for the instance endpoint to become
    95	   available (polling in `anvil_ec2_provision`).
    96	6. **No automated end-to-end tests** — validation is manual
    97	   ([`anvil/docs/VALIDATION.md`](anvil/docs/VALIDATION.md)).
    98	
    99	### Honesty note
   100	The full Anvil stack was **not executed against a live Docker or AWS
   101	environment** in this build. All code is written and reviewed against the actual
   102	artifacts, but runtime validation (container bring-up, real AWS provisioning)
   103	has not been performed here.

   114	{"returncode":0,"stdout":"Reason: Varying from the original blueprints.","stderr":""}     1	# PHASE CORE-01: Polyrepo Orchestrator
     2	
     3	## Tier
     4	Core (Foundational Infrastructure)
     5	
     6	## Component Name
     7	Polyrepo Orchestrator (The "Loom")
     8	
     9	## Description
    10	A central PHP-driven automation tool that manages the lifecycle of the Sovereign Stack polyrepo. It monitors CI status across distributed repositories, enforces merge gates, calculates Semantic Versioning (SemVer) bumps, and orchestrates unified releases. It eliminates developer overhead by automating the "Core -> Hub -> Spoke" dependency flow.
    11	
    12	## Context7 Research
    13	- **PSR Compliance**: Adheres to PSR-12 for CLI output and internal structure.
    14	- **Git Operations**: Utilizes standard `git` binary calls or a PHP Git wrapper (e.g., `czproject/git-php`).
    15	- **SemVer Logic**: Implements the Semantic Versioning 2.0.0 specification.
    16	- **Patterns**: Command Pattern for automation tasks, Observer Pattern for CI monitoring.
    17	
    18	## Architectural Design
    19	The Orchestrator is a standalone PHP executable (extending `cli/test.php` patterns).
    20	
    21	### Core Components:
    22	- **RepoManager**: Handles git cloning, branching, and tagging.
    23	- **CIMonitor**: Interfaces with GitHub Actions/GitLab CI APIs to verify build health.
    24	- **DependencyGraph**: Resolves the tier hierarchy (Core must pass before Hub).
    25	- **VersionBumpEngine**: Analyzes commit messages (Conventional Commits) to determine Major/Minor/Patch increments.
    26	
    27	### Mermaid Diagram
    28	```mermaid
    29	graph TD
    30	    A[Developer Push] --> B[CI Pipeline per Repo]
    31	    B --> C{Loom Orchestrator}
    32	    C -->|All Pass| D[Calculate SemVer Bump]
    33	    D --> E[Tag & Release]
    34	    E --> F[Trigger Downstream Tier CI]
    35	    C -->|Fail| G[Block Merge & Notify]
    36	```
    37	
    38	## Integration Strategy
    39	- **Upward**: None (this is the root).
    40	- **Downward**: Every subsequent repository in the stack must register its CI webhook with the Orchestrator.
    41	- **Environment**: Requires PHP 8.3+ CLI and Git 2.40+.
    42	
    43	## CI Verification Criteria
    44	- **Execution Speed**: Full dependency check for 10 repos must complete in < 2 seconds.
    45	- **Accuracy**: Tagging must never overwrite an existing version.
    46	- **Safety**: Merge gates must fail if a single test in a "Core" repo is red.
    47	
    48	## SemVer Impact
    49	**Major**. This establishes the fundamental repository architecture and automation layer.     1	# Phase ID: CORE-01
     2	## Tier: Core
     3	## Component Name and Description: Foundational Bootstrapper & Kernel
     4	
     5	The [`Kernel`](blueprints/CORE-01.md) and [`Bootstrapper`](blueprints/CORE-01.md) serve as the entry and initialization layer of the rebooted Sovereign Stack. It prepares the PHP 8.2+ runtime environment, sets up global error and exception handlers, detects the environment stage (e.g. development, production), loads critical system settings, and coordinates the sequential boot process of all other Core tier systems. It guarantees a sub-1ms boot baseline by avoiding external user-land dependencies and leveraging native PHP features.
     6	
     7	---
     8	
     9	## Context7 Research
    10	### 1. PSR Standards Reference
    11	- **No direct PSR standard** governs kernel bootstrapping or error handlers, but the bootstrapper must prepare the environment for PSR-11 and PSR-7 components which follow.
    12	- Integrates with standard PHP logging which aligns with **PSR-3 (Logger Interface)** to allow error/exception output handling.
    13	
    14	### 2. PHP 8.2+ Best Practices
    15	- **Readonly Classes & Properties**: All bootstrapper state (like environment, directories) should be represented via immutable data structures using PHP's native `readonly` properties.
    16	- **OPcache Preloading**: Design the Kernel with strict file structures so that it is fully compatible with preloading (`opcache.preload`), enabling high-performance, warm-state execution.
    17	- **Native Error Capturing**: Utilize `set_error_handler()`, `set_exception_handler()`, and `register_shutdown_function()` to convert all legacy PHP errors, warnings, and notices into throwables ([`Sovereign\Core\Exceptions\FatalThrowableError`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:65)).
    18	
    19	### 3. Design Patterns
    20	- **Singleton Pattern / Registry Pattern**: The running application environment is accessed via a global, thread-safe execution context or static instance manager to avoid global variable state pollution.
    21	- **Chain of Responsibility**: Bootstrapping executes a ordered array of bootstrapper stages (e.g., config load, container compilation, route compilation).
    22	
    23	---
    24	
    25	## Architectural Design
    26	
    27	### Class & Interface Structure
    28	
    29	The core Bootstrapper subsystem is represented by the following classes:
    30	
    31	1. **[`Sovereign\Core\Kernel`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:45)**: The central orchestrator managing the system life cycle.
    32	2. **[`Sovereign\Core\Bootstrapper`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:85)**: Abstract base or runner that sequentially triggers boot stages.
    33	3. **[`Sovereign\Core\Environment`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:110)**: Strict typed enum representing `DEVELOPMENT`, `TESTING`, `STAGING`, and `PRODUCTION`.
    34	4. **[`Sovereign\Core\ErrorHandler`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:135)**: Global error and exception hook registry.
    35	
    36	```php
    37	namespace Sovereign\Core;
    38	
    39	/**
    40	 * Kernel orchestration interface.
    41	 */
    42	interface KernelInterface
    43	{
    44	    public function boot(): void;
    45	    public function handleRequest(): void;
    46	    public function shutdown(): void;
    47	}
    48	```
    49	
    50	```php
    51	namespace Sovereign\Core;
    52	
    53	enum Environment: string
    54	{
    55	    case DEVELOPMENT = 'development';
    56	    case TESTING = 'testing';
    57	    case STAGING = 'staging';
    58	    case PRODUCTION = 'production';
    59	}
    60	```
    61	
    62	```php
    63	namespace Sovereign\Core;
    64	
    65	use Throwable;
    66	
    67	final class ErrorHandler
    68	{
    69	    public static function register(Environment $environment): self
    70	    {
    71	        $handler = new self($environment);
    72	        set_error_handler([$handler, 'handleError']);
    73	        set_exception_handler([$handler, 'handleException']);
    74	        register_shutdown_function([$handler, 'handleShutdown']);
    75	        return $handler;
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    private function __construct(private Environment $environment) {}
    79	
    80	    public function handleError(int $level, string $message, string $file, int $line): bool
    81	    {
    82	        if (!(error_reporting() & $level)) {
    83	            return false;
    84	        }
    85	        throw new \ErrorException($message, 0, $level, $file, $line);
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    public function handleException(Throwable $exception): void
    89	    {
    90	        // Suppress or format output based on environment
    91	        $this->logException($exception);
    92	        
    93	        if ($this->environment === Environment::PRODUCTION) {
    94	            http_response_code(500);
    95	            echo json_encode(['error' => 'Internal Server Error']);
    96	            exit(1);
    97	        }
    98	
    99	        http_response_code(500);
   100	        echo json_encode([
   101	            'error' => $exception->getMessage(),
   102	            'trace' => $exception->getTraceAsString(),
   103	            'file' => $exception->getFile(),
   104	            'line' => $exception->getLine()
   105	        ]);
   106	        exit(1);
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    public function handleShutdown(): void
   110	    {
   111	        $error = error_get_last();
   112	        if ($error !== null && in_array($error['type'], [E_ERROR, E_PARSE, E_CORE_ERROR, E_COMPILE_ERROR])) {
   113	            $this->handleException(new \ErrorException(
   114	                $error['message'],
   115	                0,
   116	                $error['type'],
   117	                $error['file'],
   118	                $error['line']
   119	            ));
   120	        }
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    private function logException(Throwable $exception): void
   124	    {
   125	        // Write raw panic log to system stream/stderr or fallback file
   126	        error_log(sprintf(
   127	            "[%s] %s in %s:%d\nStack trace:\n%s",
   128	            date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
   129	            $exception->getMessage(),
   130	            $exception->getFile(),
   131	            $exception->getLine(),
   132	            $exception->getTraceAsString()
   133	        ));
   134	    }
   135	}
   136	```
   137	
   138	### System Boot Sequence Diagram
   139	
   140	```mermaid
   141	sequenceDiagram
   142	    autonumber
   143	    participant Server as Web Server (CGI/Swoole)
   144	    participant Index as public/index.php
   145	    participant Kernel as Sovereign\Core\Kernel
   146	    participant ErrorHandler as Sovereign\Core\ErrorHandler
   147	    participant Config as Sovereign\Core\ConfigLoader
   148	    participant Container as Sovereign\Core\Container
   149	
   150	    Server->>Index: Execute
   151	    Index->>Kernel: Instantiate(basePath, env)
   152	    Kernel->>ErrorHandler: register(env)
   153	    ErrorHandler-->>Kernel: ErrorHandler registered
   154	    Kernel->>Config: load(basePath)
   155	    Config-->>Kernel: Configuration Array
   156	    Kernel->>Container: initialize(Config)
   157	    Container-->>Kernel: Container Compiled & Ready
   158	    Kernel->>Index: Boot Complete (Ready to route)
   159	```
   160	
   161	---
   162	
   163	## Integration Strategy
   164	
   165	As the absolute lowest software level in the Core tier, [`Sovereign\Core\Kernel`](blueprints/CORE-01.md:45) has zero user-land software dependencies below it.
   166	
   167	- It directly interfaces with **Swoole** or **PHP-FPM** SAPI.
   168	- It supplies the raw directory path environment context and handles root errors before [`Sovereign\Core\Container`](blueprints/CORE-02.md) or [`Sovereign\Core\ConfigLoader`](blueprints/CORE-06.md) can be safely constructed.
   169	- [`CORE-02`](blueprints/CORE-02.md) and [`CORE-06`](blueprints/CORE-06.md) are initialized *by* this Kernel, making them downstream targets.
   170	
   171	---
   172	
   173	## CI Verification Criteria
   174	
   175	### 1. Test Coverage
   176	- **Unit Tests**: Must cover environment detection, boot sequences, error handler register, and handler catch overrides with 100% path coverage.
   177	- **Simulation**: Mock custom catastrophic fatal throwables to verify exit statuses match expectations across all `Environment` stages.
   178	
   179	### 2. Performance Benchmarks
   180	- **Boot Execution Time**: Must initialize and register handlers in **< 0.15ms** on PHP 8.2 with OPcache preloading.
   181	- **Memory Overhead**: Absolute memory footprints for raw kernel boot must not exceed **1.2 MB**.
   182	
   183	---
   184	
   185	## SemVer Impact
   186	
   187	- **Major Bump** (v1.0.0-core.1): This component marks the death of the legacy monolith's framework bootloader and establishes the zero-Node reboot core lifecycle. All existing code must migrate to the new `Kernel` bootstrap entrypoint.

Global Configuration & Feature Flags\n\n## Tier\nHub (Shared Services)\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Hub Config & Flags\n\n## Description\nA global configuration management service that extends `CORE-10` to support multi-tenant configurations, dynamic feature flags, and remote settings. This allows Hub-level services and Spoke applications to toggle functionality in real-time without code redeployments.\n\n## Sequencing Rationale\nThis is the first Hub-tier phase because all subsequent Hub services (Identity, Cache, Asset Pipeline) require a unified way to retrieve shared settings and toggle environment-specific features.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-10: Config & Env Loader`, `CORE-02: DI Container`.\n- **Feature Flag Patterns**: Implements \"Kill Switches,\" \"Release Toggles,\" and \"Percentage Rollouts.\"\n- **Storage**: Uses `CORE-10` for static files and `CORE-19` (Database) for dynamic tenant-specific overrides.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **HubConfigRegistry**: Extends the Core registry to merge global defaults with tenant-specific overrides.\n- **FeatureFlagManager**: Evaluates toggle states based on context (User, Tenant, Environment).\n- **FlagEvaluator**: Handles complex rules (e.g., \"Enabled for 10% of users in region 'US'\").\n\n### Feature Flag Example\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Config;\n\ninterface FeatureManagerInterface\n{\n    public function isEnabled(string $flag, ?Context $context = null): bool;\n    public function getVariant(string $flag, ?Context $context = null): string;\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### GlobalConfigInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface GlobalConfigInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Get a configuration value with tenant-aware fallback.\n     */\n    public function get(string $key, mixed $default = null, ?string $tenantId = null): mixed;\n\n    /**\n     * Check if a feature flag is active for the current context.\n     */\n    public function feature(string $flag): bool;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Consumes `CORE-10`.\n- **Downward**: Injected as a singleton into all Hub and Spoke service providers.\n- **Contract**: Spoke applications use the `GlobalConfigInterface` to access both static settings and dynamic feature states.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Merge Logic**: Tenant overrides must never \"leak\" into the global configuration pool.\n- **Performance**: Flag evaluation for a simple boolean toggle must take < 0.005ms.\n- **Consistency**: A change in the database for a dynamic flag must reflect in the service within 1 second (cache invalidation check).\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Extends the configuration capabilities of the stack.\n\n=== ISPOKE-01 ===\n# PHASE ISPOKE-01: Administration Panel and Control Centre\n\n## Tier\nInternal Spoke (Staff-only Application)\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Command Center\n\n## Description\nThe primary administrative interface for the Sovereign Stack. It defines the \"Strict Boundary\" that ensures no internal implementation details, staff-only services, or raw internal data structures are ever exposed to the public-facing ecosystem.\n\n## Sequencing Rationale\nActs as the transition point between the completed Internal Spoke sub-tier and the upcoming External Spoke sub-tier. It must be established before any External Spoke is built to ensure boundary compliance from day zero.\n\n## Context7 Research\n### Direct Hub Dependencies\n- `HUB-08: API Gateway & Public Surface`\n- `HUB-15: Health Check & Service Discovery`\n- `HUB-16: Hub-level Orchestration Hooks`\n- `HUB-06: Audit Log & Activity Tracker`\n- `HUB-04: Global Identity & Authentication`\n\n### Transitive Core Dependencies\n- `CORE-01: Polyrepo Orchestrator (Enforcement Logic)`\n- `CORE-18: Core Kernel & Lifecycle`\n- `CORE-09: Cryptography & Hashing (Payload Verification)`\n- `CORE-06: Router (Gateway Routing)`\n\n## Architectural Design: The Strict Boundary Policy\nThe Bridge enforces a \"Default Deny\" posture for all cross-tier interactions.\n\n### 1. Data Transformation Rule\nNo Internal Spoke service or database contract may be directly exposed. All data crossing from Internal to External must be transformed into a \"Public-Safe\" Data Transfer Object (DTO) at the Bridge.\n\n### 2. Authentication Re-validation\nAny authentication context established within the Internal sub-tier must be re-validated at the Bridge before being honored on the External side. Internal \"Staff\" sessions have zero authority in the External tier.\n\n### 3. Audit Mandate\nEvery event, payload, or service call crossing the boundary is logged through `HUB-06` with a specialized \"Tier-Crossing\" metadata flag.\n\n### 4. Permitted Contract Allowlist\nThe Bridge maintains a strict registry of permitted crossing contracts. Unlisted interactions are blocked and surfaced as \"Critical Violations\" via `HUB-15`.\n\n### Boundary Flow Diagram\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant I as Internal Spoke (ISPOKE-XX)\n    participant B as BRIDGE-01 (The Vanguard)\n    participant E as External Spoke (ESPOKE-XX)\n    participant A as HUB-06 (Audit Log)\n\n    E->>B: Request for Data/Action\n    B->>B: Re-validate External Auth\n    B->>I: Internal Service Call (Secure)\n    I-->>B: Internal Data Payload\n    B->>B: Transform to Public-Safe DTO\n    B->>A: Log Crossing Event\n    B-->>E: Public-Safe Response\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### BoundaryContractInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Bridge\\Contracts;\n\ninterface BoundaryContractInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Define a permitted crossing contract.\n     */\n    public function registerContract(string $contractId, DTOTransformerInterface $transformer): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Enforce the boundary for an incoming request.\n     */\n    public function enforce(RequestInterface $request): ResponseInterface;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy (Formal Policy)\n- **Runtime Enforcement**: The Bridge uses `HUB-08` middleware to intercept all cross-tier traffic.\n- **Service Discovery**: Consumes `HUB-15` to identify legitimate Internal endpoints while hiding them from External visibility.\n- **Orchestration**: Integrated with `HUB-16` to block all boundary-crossing traffic during \"Critical Maintenance\" windows.\n- **Reporting**: Any attempted violation of the boundary (e.g., direct DB access attempt from an ESPOKE) triggers an immediate \"P0 Violation Alert\" in `HUB-15`.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Zero-Exposure Test**: Automated scans must verify that no PHP class in the `Sovereign\\External` namespace imports any class from the `Sovereign\\Internal` namespace.\n- **Transformation Latency**: DTO transformation and audit logging must add no more than 2ms to the total request lifecycle.\n- **Violation Response**: Attempting to call an unregistered contract must return a `403 Forbidden` within 5ms.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Establishes the foundational security and architectural integrity of the entire platform's public interface.\n","stderr":""}{"returncode":0,"stdout":"=== PhasedBlueprints/README ===\n# 81-Phased Executive Summary: The \"Pure Superpowers\" Vision\nDGLab represents a paradigm shift in web application development. While the industry has gravitated toward increasingly complex, multi-layered \"Frankenstein\" stacks, DGLab has engineered a **Unified Engine** that delivers high-performance, reactive user experiences with a fraction of the traditional overhead.\n\nOur \"Pure Superpowers\" directive is more than a technical choice; it is a business strategy designed to maximize **Operational Velocity** while minimizing **Maintenance Debt**.\n\n---\n\n## 2. The Hub-and-Spoke Value Model\nAt the heart of DGLab is a **Hub-and-Spoke** architecture. Imagine a central control tower (The Hub) managing global security, navigation, and brand identity, while specialized business engines (The Spokes) execute domain-specific tasks like AI generation, content management, or data analysis.\n\n### Simplified Value Flow\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    User((The Market)) --> Hub[Central Hub: CMS Studio]\n    Hub --> Security[Enterprise Trust Layer]\n    Hub --> Business[Business Value Spokes]\n    Business --> MangaScript[AI Orchestration]\n    Business --> Media[Media Processing]\n    Business --> Custom[New Growth Domains]\n\n    style Hub fill:#f9f,stroke:#333,stroke-width:4px\n    style Security fill:#bbf,stroke:#333\n    style Business fill:#dfd,stroke:#333\n```\n\n**Why this matters for investors:**\n*   **Modular Expansion:** Adding a new revenue stream (a \"Spoke\") does not require rebuilding the foundation.\n*   **Centralized Risk Management:** Security and compliance are enforced at the Hub, protecting all underlying assets simultaneously.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Operational Efficiency: The \"Node-Free\" Advantage\nDGLab is unique in its **Node-free architecture**. By eliminating the need for external JavaScript runtimes and complex build pipelines, we have reduced the \"moving parts\" of the application by over 40%.\n\n### Complexity vs. Performance\n| Metric | Industry Standard (Legacy) | DGLab (Superpowers) | Business Impact |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| **Boot Time** | 100ms - 500ms | **< 5ms** | Instant user retention. |\n| **Build Layers** | Node, Webpack, Babel, PHP | **Internal PHP Engine** | Reduced DevOps cost. |\n| **Maintenance** | High (Vulnerable Dependencies) | **Low (Lean Core)** | Lower long-term TCO. |\n\n**The Bottom Line:** We spend less on infrastructure and maintenance, allowing us to pivot 100% of our resources toward feature innovation.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Security & The \"Fortress of Reliability\"\nSecurity is not an afterthought; it is baked into the binary. Our **EncryptionService** is designed for the future, featuring:\n*   **Post-Quantum Readiness:** Protecting data against future computing threats.\n*   **Absolute Isolation:** Multi-tenancy logic ensures that data from one partner never touches another.\n*   **High-Fidelity Auditing:** Every critical action is recorded in a forensic-grade audit trail, providing transparency and trust for enterprise partners.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Strategic Roadmap & Market Readiness\nDGLab is built on a **Phased Evolution** model. Our roadmap is not a list of wishes, but a meticulously engineered sequence of 18-phase implementations that ensure stability at every step.\n\n### The Path Forward\n1.  **Foundation (Complete):** Core framework and high-speed routing.\n2.  **Expansion (Active):** Integrating AI Orchestration and Visual Content Studios.\n3.  **Saturation (Upcoming):** Global search, AI-assisted automation, and cross-platform synchronization.\n\n## 6. Conclusion\nDGLab is more than a codebase; it is a scalable, secure, and highly efficient **Business Operating System**. By choosing performance over bloat and simplicity over complexity, we have created an asset that is uniquely positioned for rapid growth and long-term sustainability.\n\n---\n\n## 7. The Technology Stack: Lean, Mean, and Scalable\nUnlike traditional web apps that require a dozen different server technologies, DGLab runs on a simplified, unified stack. This \"Vertical Integration\" allows us to ship features faster and with higher quality.\n\n### The Unified Stack vs. The Fragmented Legacy\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph Legacy_Stack [Traditional Multi-Layer Stack]\n        L1[Node.js Runtime]\n        L2[Build Pipelines: Webpack/Babel]\n        L3[Client Frameworks: React/Vue]\n        L4[Server Logic: PHP/Python]\n        L1 --> L2 --> L3 <--> L4\n    end\n\n    subgraph Superpowers_Stack [The DGLab Unified Engine]\n        S1[Core PHP Framework]\n        S2[Internal Asset Bundler]\n        S3[Reactive SuperPHP UI]\n        S1 --> S2\n        S1 --> S3\n    end\n\n    style Legacy_Stack fill:#fee,stroke:#f66\n    style Superpowers_Stack fill:#efe,stroke:#6f6\n```\n\n=== DETAILED_SYSTEM_ANALYSIS ===\n# DGLab: Detailed System Analysis & Phased Planning\n\n## 1. Architectural Philosophy: The \"Pure Superpowers\" Ecosystem\nDGLab is built on a \"Pure Superpowers\" directive, which mandates a high-performance, Node-free, and reactive-first architecture.\n\n### Key Principles:\n- **Node-Free Development**: All front-end assets (JS/SCSS) are managed and bundled by the server-side `AssetBundler` (WebpackService).\n- **Reactive PHP (SuperPHP)**: UI components use server-side diffing and DOM morphing, eliminating the need for complex client-side frameworks.\n- **Hub-and-Spoke Unification**: CMS Studio serves as the \"Hub\" (Single Pane of Glass), while specialized services (MangaScript, AuthService, etc.) serve as \"Spokes\".\n- **Absolute Isolation**: 100% filesystem and database isolation in testing to ensure a \"Fortress of Reliability\".\n\n---\n\n## 2. Codebase State Analysis (Oct 2026)\n\n### 2.1 Core Framework (100% COMPLETED)\n- **Application (IoC)**: Advanced auto-wiring with reflection, lazy-loading, and comprehensive `flush()` logic.\n- **Router & Middleware**: Recursive \"Onion\" model with regex matching and O(1) matching roadmap.\n- **SuperPHP Engine**: Lexer/Parser/Compiler pipeline supporting reactive components, named slots, and lifecycle hooks.\n- **Superpowers SPA**: Navigation engine with fragments, morphing, and PWA capabilities.\n- **EventDispatcher**: Dot-notation events with Sync and Queue drivers.\n\n### 2.2 Foundation Services (100% COMPLETED)\n- **AuthService**: Multi-guard (Session, JWT, Token) with MFA and tenant-aware RBAC.\n- **DownloadService**: Secure delivery with signed URLs and audit trails.\n- **AssetBundler**: Pure PHP asset pipeline with minification and hashing.\n- **Nexus**: Swoole-based WebSocket service with Redis Pub/Sub scaling.\n\n### 2.3 Studio Apps (IN PROGRESS)\n- **CMS Studio**: Phases 1-2 (IAM & Tenancy) are complete. Phases 3-9 (Architect, Content, Pulse, etc.) are in the blueprint stage.\n- **MangaScript**: Phase 1 (Core Engine) complete. Phase 2 (AI Orchestration) is meticulously detailed.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Meticulously Phased Roadmap (Next Priorities)\n\n### Phase 3.1: CMS Studio Architect (The Schema Engine)\n- **Goal**: Implement a visual schema builder for content types.\n- **Technical**: Hybrid EAV storage model, dynamic model generation.\n- **Superpowers**: Reactive forms for field definition.\n\n### Phase 3.2: MangaScript AI Orchestration (Vision & Streaming)\n- **Goal**: Add multi-modal support and real-time generation.\n- **Technical**: `AIStreamingResponse` integration, Vision-model routing.\n- **Observability**: Cost and latency auditing per generation.\n\n### Phase 3.3: Test Suite Expansion (E2E & Visual)\n- **Goal**: 100% coverage for the SPA navigation and reactive components.\n- **Technical**: Symfony Panther for headless browser automation.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Operational Strategy (Setup & Deployment)\n\n### 4.1 Development Stage (The Forge)\n1. **Bootstrap**: `composer install`, `.env` configuration.\n2. **Database Forge**: `php cli/migrate.php run` to initialize SQLite/MySQL.\n3. **Asset Forge**: `php cli/build-assets.php` to bundle JS/CSS without Node.\n4. **Nexus Forge**: `php cli/nexus.php start` for real-time features.\n5. **Validation**: `vendor/bin/phpunit` for the \"Fortress of Reliability\".\n\n### 4.2 Production Stage (The Fortress)\n1. **Infrastucture**: PHP 8.3-FPM + Nginx + Redis + MySQL/Postgres.\n2. **Deployment**: Atomic execution via `cli/deploy.php` (Check -> Migrate -> Build -> Optimize -> Health).\n3. **Nexus Supervision**: Systemd + Nginx WSS Proxying.\n4. **Hardening**: SSL enforcement, `APP_DEBUG=false`, Redis-backed sessions.\n\n### 4.3 Maintenance Stage (The Pulse)\n1. **Monitoring**: `AuditService` forensics, Monolog rotation, Nexus status checks.\n2. **Scaling**: Horizontal scaling of Web and Nexus nodes via Redis state sharing.\n3. **Recovery**: Automated DB dumps, S3-synced uploads, atomic rollback via Git.\n\n---\n\n=== HUB_AND_SPOKE (origin root) ===\n# Hub-and-Spoke Architecture Specification\n\n## 1. Overview\nThe DGLab ecosystem follows a **Hub-and-Spoke** model. This architecture consolidates all web-accessible front-end concerns into a single central service (The Hub) while delegating domain-specific logic to modular, independent services (The Spokes).\n\n## 2. The Hub (CMS Studio)\nThe Hub is the only service exposed directly to the public Internet via the main application domain.\n\n### Responsibilities:\n- **Routing & Navigation**: Intercepting and handling all browser-initiated navigation.\n- **UI/UX**: Providing the global layout, navigation, and the SuperPHP component library.\n- **Security**: Centrally enforcing Auth and Tenancy across all requests.\n- **State Persistence**: Managing global state and reactive fragments for a seamless SPA experience.\n- **Service Orchestration**: Dispatching requests to the appropriate Spokes and rendering the results.\n\n## 3. The Spokes (`app/Spokes/`)\nSpokes are domain-specific engines that implement the core business logic of the ecosystem.\n\n### Key Principles:\n- **Internal Isolation**: Spokes do not define their own web routes or views.\n- **Data-Oriented**: Spokes return data (or pre-compiled fragments) to the Hub for final presentation.\n- **Service Inheritance**: All spokes extend a `BaseSpokeService` that provides access to the core framework (Database, Auth, Tenancy).\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    User((User/Browser)) --> Hub[Hub: CMS Studio]\n    Hub --> Auth[AuthService]\n    Hub --> Tenancy[TenancyService]\n    Hub --> EventBus[Global Event Bus]\n    Hub --> Spoke1[Spoke: MangaScript]\n    Hub --> Spoke2[Spoke: EpubFontChanger]\n    Hub --> Spoke3[Spoke: MediaLibrary]\n    Spoke1 --> AI[AI Orchestration]\n    Spoke2 --> Files[File Processing]\n```\n\n## 4. Interaction Patterns\n\n### A. Direct Delegation\nThe Hub calls a Spoke service method directly from a controller action.\n```php\npublic function convert(Request $request)\n{\n    $spoke = Application::getInstance()->get(MangaScriptSpoke::class);\n    $result = $spoke->process($request->all());\n    return View::render('mangascript.result', ['data' => $result]);\n}\n```\n\n### B. Event-Driven Communication\nThe Hub emits a \"Command\" event, and one or more Spokes react asynchronously.\n```php\n// In Hub Controller\nevent(new \\DGLab\\Events\\Spoke\\ProcessRequested($payload));\n\n// In Spoke Subscriber\npublic function onProcessRequested(Event $event)\n{\n    $this->executeTask($event->payload);\n    event(new \\DGLab\\Events\\Spoke\\ProcessCompleted($result));\n}\n```\n\n### C. Reactive Fragment Update\nSpokes can provide specialized SuperPHP components that the Hub \"mounts\" into the main layout.\n```html\n<s:hub_layout>\n    <s:spoke_mangascript_workspace :session=\"$session\" />\n</s:hub_layout>\n```\n\n## 5. UI Synchronization\nSpokes do not have their own CSS/JS files. All styling must utilize:\n1. **Global Bootstrap/Tailwind classes** provided by the Hub.\n2. **Standard Hub Components** (`<s:ui_button>`, `<s:ui_modal>`, etc.).\n3. **SuperPHP directives** (`@global`, `@persist`) to stay in sync with the Hub's reactive state.\n\n## 6. History & Evolution\n- **Phase 1 (Legacy Separation)**: Services like MangaScript and EpubFontChanger existed as independent, monolithic scripts.\n- **Phase 2 (Hub Foundations)**: CMS Studio was established as the central administration and navigation portal.\n- **Phase 3 (Spoke Refactoring)**: Legacy services were refactored into classes within `app/Spokes/` and stripped of their independent routing logic.\n- **Phase 4 (UI Unification)**: All spoke-related views were converted to SuperPHP fragments and migrated to the Hub's component library.\n\n## 7. Future Roadmap\n- **Phase 5: Automated Spoke Discovery (M)**: A system that automatically registers Spokes and their capabilities into the Hub's navigation menu.\n- **Phase 6: Spoke Sandboxing (XL)**: Running each Spoke in its own isolated process or container to prevent resource exhaustion from impacting the Hub.\n- **Phase 7: Centralized Spoke Logs (S)**: A unified dashboard in CMS Studio to monitor the health and performance of all active Spokes.\n\n## 8. Validation\n### Success Criteria\n- **Independence**: A Spoke can be added or removed without modifying the core Hub routing table.\n- **UX**: Navigating from Hub to Spoke UI must feel instantaneous (SPA fragments).\n- **Security**: Every Spoke-initiated action must be traceable back to a Hub-authenticated user and tenant.\n\n### Verification Steps\n- [ ] Verify that disabling a Spoke service does not crash the Hub's main layout.\n- [ ] Confirm that `X-Tenant-Id` is correctly propagated from Hub to Spoke.\n- [ ] Run a cross-spoke event test to ensure the global `EventDispatcher` correctly routes messages between Spokes.\n","stderr":""}{"returncode":0,"stdout":"=== ComponentBlueprints/STABILIZATION_ROADMAP ===\n# DGLab Ecosystem: Stabilization & Implementation Roadmap\n\nThis document provides a consolidated, high-level summary of all remaining implementation phases across the DGLab blueprints. It introduces \"Cache Tags\" for bulk invalidation and \"Atomic Locks\" for preventing race conditions in distributed environments.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-15: Cache Abstraction`, `CORE-02: DI Container`.\n- **Patterns**: Cache-Aside, Write-Through, and Distributed Locking (Redlock-inspired).\n- **Drivers**: Primarily Redis for distributed state, with fallback to PSR-16 Local Cache.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **HubCacheManager**: Factory that provides tagged cache instances.\n- **TaggableStore**: Wraps PSR-16 stores to support tag-based invalidation.\n- **LockManager**: Provides mutex locks to prevent \"cache stampedes\" and ensure single-execution of critical tasks.\n\n### Distributed Lock Example\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Cache;\n\ninterface LockInterface\n{\n    public function acquire(string $name, int $seconds = 0): bool;\n    public function release(string $name): void;\n    public function block(string $name, int $seconds, callable $callback): mixed;\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### CacheInterface (Hub Extension)\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\nuse Psr\\SimpleCache\\CacheInterface as PsrCache;\n\ninterface HubCacheInterface extends PsrCache\n{\n    /**\n     * Return a cache instance scoped to specific tags.\n     */\n    public function tags(array $tags): self;\n\n    /**\n     * Invalidate all items associated with the given tags.\n     */\n    public function flushTags(array $tags): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Get an atomic lock instance.\n     */\n    public function lock(string $name, int $seconds = 0): LockInterface;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Consumes `CORE-15`.\n- **Downward**: Used by `HUB-04` (Identity) for session storage and `HUB-07` (Rate Limiter) for bucket tracking.\n- **Contract**: Spoke applications interact with `HubCacheInterface` for all performance-related data persistence.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Atomic Integrity**: Locks must never be acquired by two concurrent processes on the same resource.\n- **Tag Isolation**: Flashing tag `A` must not affect items associated only with tag `B`.\n- **Performance**: Tag-based retrieval should add < 0.1ms overhead compared to raw PSR-16 `get()`.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Adds advanced caching features to the foundational abstraction.\n\n=== HUB-03 ===\n# PHASE HUB-03: Shared Asset Pipeline\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Asset Engine\n\n## Description\nA custom, PHP-only asset pipeline (the \"Unified Engine\") for processing frontend resources. It handles CSS minification, JavaScript concatenation/wrapping, asset fingerprinting (cache busting), and versioned manifest generation. It operates entirely within the PHP runtime, eliminating the need for Node.js, npm, or Webpack.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-14: Filesystem`, `CORE-10: Config`.\n- **PHP Libraries**: Evaluates `matthiasmullie/minify` for CSS/JS compression and `leafo/scssphp` if SCSS support is required (recommending sovereign implementation for minification).\n- **Patterns**: Pipeline/Filter pattern for asset transformations.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **AssetBundler**: The main engine that discovers source files and orchestrates the build.\n- **ManifestGenerator**: Creates a JSON map of source filenames to their fingerprinted versions.\n- **Minifier**: A set of PHP-based regex filters that strip comments and whitespace from CSS/JS.\n- **AssetServer**: A development utility that serves assets with live-reloading hooks (referencing CORE-18 Kernel hooks).\n\n### Asset Flow\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    Src[resources/css/app.css] --> B[Bundler]\n    B --> M[Minifier]\n    M --> F[Fingerprinter]\n    F --> Out[public/assets/app.a1b2c3.css]\n    F --> Manifest[manifest.json]\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### AssetManagerInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface AssetManagerInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Get the URL for a versioned asset.\n     */\n    public function url(string $path): string;\n\n    /**\n     * Build the asset manifest (production only).\n     */\n    public function build(): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Register a new asset transformation filter.\n     */\n    public function addFilter(AssetFilterInterface $filter): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Uses `CORE-14` for I/O operations.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use a `@asset('css/app.css')` SuperPHP directive (extending CORE-12) which resolves through this Hub service.\n- **Non-Node Requirement**: All logic must be pure PHP. No `shell_exec('npm ...')` allowed.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Determinism**: Building the same asset twice with no changes must produce the same fingerprint.\n- **Minification**: Final CSS size must be reduced by at least 30% compared to unminified source.\n- **Integrity**: The `manifest.json` must always match the files currently present in the `public/assets` directory.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Establishes the frontend build strategy for the entire ecosystem.\n\n=== HUB-04 ===\n# PHASE HUB-04: Global Identity & Authentication\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Identity\n\n## Description\nA comprehensive identity management and authentication service. It provides user lifecycle management, session handling, secure password hashing, and a foundation for OAuth2/OIDC. It centralizes authentication logic so Spoke applications can verify user identity through a unified Hub contract.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-19: DBAL`, `CORE-16: Encryption`, `HUB-02: Cache`.\n- **Standards**: RFC 6749 (OAuth 2.0), OpenID Connect.\n- **Reference**: Evaluates `league/oauth2-server` but recommends a sovereign implementation for the core session/token logic to ensure sub-5ms verification.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **AuthManager**: Coordinates authentication attempts across multiple guards (Session, Token, API Key).\n- **UserRepositoryInterface**: Abstraction for user data storage (database by default).\n- **SessionStore**: Managed via `HUB-02` (Cache) to ensure stateless horizontal scaling.\n- **TokenService**: Generates and validates cryptographically signed JWTs or opaque tokens.\n\n### Authentication Flow\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant U as User\n    participant A as AuthManager\n    participant G as SessionGuard\n    participant D as DBAL\n    U->>A: login(credentials)\n    A->>D: findUserByEmail(email)\n    A->>A: verifyPassword(hash)\n    A->>G: login(user)\n    G->>D: createSession()\n    A-->>U: Success (Cookie/Token)\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### AuthInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface AuthInterface\n{\n    public function attempt(array $credentials): bool;\n    public function login(Authenticatable $user, bool $remember = false): void;\n    public function logout(): void;\n    public function check(): bool;\n    public function user(): ?Authenticatable;\n    public function id(): mixed;\n}\n```\n\n### Authenticatable (Abstract)\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Auth;\n\nabstract class Authenticatable\n{\n    abstract public function getAuthIdentifier(): mixed;\n    abstract public function getAuthPassword(): string;\n    abstract public function getRememberToken(): ?string;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Consumes `CORE-19` for persistence and `CORE-16` for sensitive data encryption.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use the `AuthInterface` to protect routes and identify users.\n- **Middleware**: Provides a Hub-level `AuthMiddleware` (extending CORE-05) for the API Gateway (`HUB-08`).\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Brute Force Resistance**: Must automatically throttle login attempts after 5 failures per IP (referencing HUB-07).\n- **Session Isolation**: A session for Tenant A must never be valid for Tenant B.\n- **Performance**: Auth check for a valid session must take < 1ms (hot cache).\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Defines the security boundary of the stack.\n\n=== HUB-05 ===\n# PHASE HUB-05: RBAC & Permission Engine\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Guardian\n\n## Description\nA fine-grained Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) and Permission Engine. It builds on `HUB-04` (Identity) to define what an authenticated user is allowed to do. It supports Roles, Permissions, and dynamic \"Abilities\" (Policies) based on resource ownership or attributes.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `HUB-04: Identity`, `CORE-19: DBAL`, `HUB-02: Cache`.\n- **Patterns**: Access Control List (ACL), Policy-Based Access Control (PBAC).\n- **Optimization**: Heavily utilizes `HUB-02` to cache user permissions and prevent repetitive DB queries.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **Gate**: The primary entry point for authorization checks.\n- **PolicyRegistry**: Maps resource types (e.g., `Document`) to specific `Policy` classes.\n- **RoleManager**: Handles assignments of permissions to roles and roles to users.\n- **PermissionLoader**: Eager-loads all relevant permissions for a user upon authentication.\n\n### Authorization Example\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Auth;\n\nclass DocumentPolicy\n{\n    public function update(User $user, Document $document): bool\n    {\n        return $user->id === $document->author_id || $user->hasRole('admin');\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### GateInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface GateInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Check if the user has a specific permission or ability.\n     */\n    public function allows(string $ability, mixed $arguments = []): bool;\n\n    /**\n     * Define a simple closure-based ability.\n     */\n    public function define(string $ability, callable $callback): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Register a resource policy class.\n     */\n    public function policy(string $class, string $policy): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Authorize an action or throw an AccessDeniedException.\n     */\n    public function authorize(string $ability, mixed $arguments = []): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Depends on `HUB-04` for the authenticated user context.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use the `@can('edit', $post)` SuperPHP directive (extending CORE-12) which calls this engine.\n- **Database**: Stores roles and permissions in a set of Hub-managed tables (`roles`, `permissions`, `role_user`, etc.).\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Deny by Default**: Any check for an undefined permission must return `false`.\n- **Cache Invalidation**: Changing a user's role in the DB must immediately reflect in the `allows()` check (cache clear logic).\n- **Depth Test**: Must handle nested roles (Role A inherits from Role B) with < 1ms overhead.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Completes the security and authorization framework.\n\n=== HUB-06 ===\n# PHASE HUB-06: Audit Log & Activity Tracker\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Auditor\n\n## Description\nA centralized logging service for tracking system-wide activity and user actions. It provides a tamper-evident record of \"who did what, and when\" across the entire polyrepo stack. It focuses on high-integrity data storage and searchable audit trails for compliance and security forensics.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-19: DBAL`, `HUB-04: Identity`, `CORE-03: Event Dispatcher`.\n- **Patterns**: Observer Pattern (via events), Event Sourcing (lite version).\n- **Integrity**: Implements row-level hashing or \"chained logs\" to detect record tampering.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **AuditManager**: Listens for system events and determines which ones require auditing.\n- **LogWriter**: Asynchronously writes audit records to a dedicated high-performance database or append-only file (referencing CORE-14).\n- **ActivityTracker**: A trait-based utility that Spoke applications can add to their models to automatically track CRUD operations.\n- **AuditViewer**: Hub-level API for querying and filtering logs by user, tenant, or action type.\n\n### Record Schema\n```json\n{\n  \"id\": \"uuid\",\n  \"user_id\": \"int\",\n  \"tenant_id\": \"int\",\n  \"action\": \"document.update\",\n  \"resource_type\": \"Document\",\n  \"resource_id\": \"123\",\n  \"changes\": {\"title\": [\"Old\", \"New\"]},\n  \"ip_address\": \"string\",\n  \"user_agent\": \"string\",\n  \"timestamp\": \"iso8601\",\n  \"signature\": \"sha256\"\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### AuditorInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface AuditorInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Manually record an audit entry.\n     */\n    public function record(string $action, ?string $resourceType = null, ?string $resourceId = null, array $metadata = []): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Search the audit trail.\n     */\n    public function search(array $criteria): array;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Consumes `CORE-03` to listen for `HubEvent` types and `CORE-19` for persistence.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use the `Auditable` trait on their domain models.\n- **Performance**: High-volume audits are queued (referencing HUB-10) to avoid blocking the user request.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Tamper Detection**: Must provide a utility that verifies the integrity of the audit chain (hashes must match).\n- **Zero-Drop Policy**: Under high load (1000 logs/sec), the system must successfully buffer and write all logs without data loss.\n- **PII Stripping**: Must verify that sensitive fields (passwords, SSNs) are automatically filtered from the `changes` payload.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Essential for enterprise-grade compliance and security.\n\n=== HUB-07 ===\n# PHASE HUB-07: Rate Limiter & Throttle Engine\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Throttle\n\n## Description\nA high-performance rate limiting and request throttling engine. It protects Hub services and Spoke applications from abuse, brute-force attacks, and API over-consumption. It implements various algorithms like Token Bucket, Leaky Bucket, and Fixed Window.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `HUB-02: Cache`, `CORE-04: HTTP Message`.\n- **Algorithms**: Token Bucket (smooth traffic), Fixed Window (simple quotas).\n- **Storage**: Strictly uses `HUB-02` (Redis/APCu) for sub-millisecond counter incrementing.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **Limiter**: Core logic for evaluating \"Allowed\" vs \"Denied\".\n- **BucketStore**: Interface for persisting counter state across requests.\n- **ThrottleMiddleware**: A PSR-15 middleware (extending CORE-05) that automatically applies limits based on route attributes.\n- **DynamicQuota**: Resolves limits based on user roles or tenant tiers (referencing HUB-05).\n\n### Rate Limit Attribute\n```php\n#[Route('/api/search', method: 'GET')]\n#[Throttle(limit: 60, per: 'minute', by: 'ip')]\npublic function search() { ... }\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### RateLimiterInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface RateLimiterInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Check if a specific key has exceeded its limit.\n     */\n    public function check(string $key, int $maxAttempts, int $decaySeconds): bool;\n\n    /**\n     * Increment the counter for a key and return the current count.\n     */\n    public function hit(string $key, int $decaySeconds): int;\n\n    /**\n     * Reset the counter for a specific key.\n     */\n    public function clear(string $key): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Get the number of remaining attempts for a key.\n     */\n    public function remaining(string $key, int $maxAttempts): int;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Consumes `HUB-02` for fast state management.\n- **Downward**: Applied globally via `HUB-08` (API Gateway) and individually in Spoke applications via middleware.\n- **HTTP Headers**: Automatically appends `X-RateLimit-Limit`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining`, and `Retry-After`.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Precision**: Must allow exactly 100 requests if the limit is 100, and deny the 101st request within the same window.\n- **Concurrency**: Must handle 10 concurrent requests for the same key without \"double-counting\" errors.\n- **Overhead**: Evaluating a rate limit must add < 0.2ms to total request time.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Enhances system stability and security.\n\n=== HUB-08 ===\n# PHASE HUB-08: API Gateway Abstraction Layer\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Gateway\n\n## Description\nA unified entry point for all API traffic in the Sovereign Stack. It serves as both an internal service mesh (governing Spoke-to-Hub communication) and a public-facing gateway for external consumers. It handles request routing, authentication translation, unified error responses, and protocol bridging.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-06: Router`, `HUB-04: Identity`, `HUB-07: Rate Limiter`, `CORE-04: HTTP Message`.\n- **Patterns**: API Gateway, Proxy, Facade.\n- **Protocol**: Primarily REST/JSON, but designed to bridge to internal RPC or Event streams.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **GatewayController**: A specialized controller that intercepts cross-tier requests.\n- **RequestTranslator**: Converts external request formats into internal service calls.\n- **ServiceRegistry**: Maps \"Service Names\" (e.g., `identity-service`) to internal URLs or class identifiers.\n- **ResponseAggregator**: Combines data from multiple Hub services into a single unified JSON response.\n\n### Internal vs External Logic\n- **Internal**: Uses secure, high-speed class resolution or internal IPC; bypasses public throttling.\n- **External**: Enforces `HUB-04` (Auth) and `HUB-07` (Throttling); maps external tokens to internal user contexts.\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### GatewayInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\nuse Psr\\Http\\Message\\ServerRequestInterface;\nuse Psr\\Http\\Message\\ResponseInterface;\n\ninterface GatewayInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Dispatch a request to an internal Hub service.\n     */\n    public function dispatchInternal(string $service, string $action, array $params = []): mixed;\n\n    /**\n     * Proxy an external request to the appropriate Spoke or Hub handler.\n     */\n    public function proxy(ServerRequestInterface $request): ResponseInterface;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Built on the `CORE-06` Router and `CORE-18` Kernel.\n- **Downward**: All Spoke applications communicate with the Hub via this Gateway to ensure auditability (`HUB-06`) and security.\n- **Security**: The Gateway is the only component that handles SSL termination and CORS validation for the Hub tier.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Latency**: Proxying a request through the gateway must add < 2ms of overhead.\n- **Error Consistency**: An error in a background Hub service must be translated into a standardized `{\"error\": \"...\", \"code\": 500}` response by the Gateway.\n- **Isolation**: A failure in the Gateway must not bring down the individual Hub services (circuit breaker pattern).\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Defines the communication interface for the entire stack.\n\n=== HUB-09 ===\n# PHASE HUB-09: Event Bus / Message Broker\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Pulse (Event Bus)\n\n## Description\nA global message broker and event bus for decoupled communication between Hub services and Spoke applications. It extends the `CORE-03` Event Dispatcher to support distributed pub/sub patterns across multiple repositories and processes.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-03: Event Dispatcher`, `HUB-02: Cache`, `HUB-10: Queue`.\n- **Patterns**: Pub/Sub, Fan-out, Event Streaming.\n- **Drivers**: Redis Pub/Sub for real-time, Database for persistent event stores.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **EventBus**: The global coordinator for cross-repository events.\n- **SubscriberRegistry**: Maintains a map of \"Interests\" (e.g., Spoke A is interested in `hub.user.created`).\n- **PulseBridge**: Connects local `CORE-03` events to the global `Pulse` bus.\n- **DeadLetterQueue**: Handles events that fail to reach their subscribers after multiple retries.\n\n### Pulse Event Lifecycle\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    S[Service A] -->|dispatch| L[Local Dispatcher]\n    L -->|bridge| P[Pulse Bus]\n    P -->|fan-out| Q1[Queue: Subscriber B]\n    P -->|fan-out| Q2[Queue: Subscriber C]\n    Q1 -->|handle| W1[Worker B]\n    Q2 -->|handle| W2[Worker C]\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### EventBusInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface EventBusInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Publish an event to the global bus.\n     */\n    public function publish(GlobalEvent $event): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Subscribe to a specific event pattern.\n     */\n    public function subscribe(string $eventPattern, callable|string $handler): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Built as a wrapper around `CORE-03`.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications register \"Global Listeners\" that respond to Hub-tier triggers (e.g., a Spoke clearing its local cache when a global setting changes in `HUB-01`).\n- **Asynchronicity**: Relies on `HUB-10` (Queue) to ensure heavy event listeners do not block the publishing service.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Delivery Guarantee**: An event published to the bus must reach all registered subscribers at least once.\n- **Fan-out Speed**: Publishing an event with 5 subscribers must complete the dispatch in < 5ms (non-blocking).\n- **Decoupling**: Subscriber B crashing must not prevent Subscriber C from receiving the event.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Essential for scalable, decoupled micro-architecture within the polyrepo.\n\n=== HUB-10 ===\n# PHASE HUB-10: Queue & Job Dispatcher\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Queue\n\n## Description\nA robust asynchronous job processing and queue management system. It allows long-running tasks (email sending, report generation, image processing) to be offloaded from the web request cycle. It supports multiple drivers, delayed jobs, retries, and job priority.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-19: DBAL`, `HUB-02: Cache`.\n- **Patterns**: Producer-Consumer, Competing Unified API for pushing jobs to various queues (Database, Redis, Sync).\n- **Worker**: A long-running CLI process (`CORE-13`) that polls the queue and executes jobs.\n- **Job**: A simple PHP class implementing a `handle()` method.\n- **FailedJobProvider**: Manages jobs that have exceeded their retry limit for manual inspection.\n\n### Job Example\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Jobs;\n\nclass SendWelcomeEmail implements JobInterface\n{\n    public function __construct(public int $userId) {}\n\n    public function handle(NotificationService $notifications): void\n    {\n        $notifications->send($this->userId, 'welcome');\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### QueueInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface QueueInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Push a new job onto the queue.\n     */\n    public function push(object $job, string $queue = 'default'): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Push a job to be executed after a delay.\n     */\n    public function later(int $delay, object $job, string $queue = 'default'): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Uses `CORE-19` for the database driver and `HUB-02` for the Redis driver.\n- **Downward**: Every Hub and Spoke service can dispatch asynchronous jobs via the `QueueInterface`.\n- **CLI**: Provides `s-cli queue:work` and `s-cli queue:retry` commands (referencing CORE-20).\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Job Isolation**: Each job must run in its own fresh process or container environment to prevent state leakage.\n- **Retry Logic**: A job that fails must be retried exactly the number of times specified in its configuration.\n- **Throughput**: The database driver must handle 500 job pushes/sec on standard hardware.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Introduces asynchronous capabilities to the entire ecosystem.\n\n","stderr":""}{"returncode":0,"stdout":"=== HUB-11 ===\n# PHASE HUB-11: File Storage Abstraction (Cloud/Multi-disk)\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Cloud Storage\n\n## Description\nAn extension of the `CORE-14` Storage Driver that adds support for cloud-based filesystems (AWS S3, Cloudflare R2, Google Cloud Storage) and introduces a multi-disk management layer. It allows applications to transparently switch between local and cloud storage via configuration.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-14: Filesystem`, `CORE-10: Config`.\n- **Drivers**: Implements S3-compatible drivers using pure PHP stream wrappers or thin SDK integrations.\n- **Patterns**: Strategy Pattern (Drivers), Factory Pattern (Disk resolution).\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **StorageManager**: Resolves named disks (e.g., `avatars`, `exports`) to specific drivers.\n- **S3Driver**: Implementation of `FilesystemInterface` targeting S3-compatible APIs.\n- **UrlSigner**: Utility for generating temporary, time-limited URLs for private cloud files.\n- **DiskSync**: A utility for migrating files between different disks (e.g., Local to S3).\n\n### Configuration Example\n```php\n'disks' => [\n    'local' => ['driver' => 'local', 'root' => '/storage'],\n    's3' => [\n        'driver' => 's3',\n        'key' => '...',\n        'secret' => '...',\n        'bucket' => 'sovereign-assets',\n    ],\n]\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### StorageInterface (Hub Extension)\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\nuse Sovereign\\Core\\Filesystem\\FilesystemInterface;\n\ninterface StorageInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Get a specific disk instance.\n     */\n    public function disk(string $name): FilesystemInterface;\n\n    /**\n     * Generate a public URL for a file.\n     */\n    public function url(string $path): string;\n\n    /**\n     * Generate a temporary signed URL.\n     */\n    public function temporaryUrl(string $path, \\DateTimeInterface $expiration): string;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Built on top of the `CORE-14` interface.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use `StorageInterface` for all user-generated content, remaining agnostic of the underlying physical storage.\n- **Service**: Injected into `HUB-03` (Asset Pipeline) to allow deploying compiled assets directly to a CDN-backed S3 bucket.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Driver Interchangeability**: A file written via the `Local` driver must be readable via the `S3` driver (given shared underlying storage or sync).\n- **Security**: Signed URLs must expire exactly at the designated time and become invalid.\n- **Streaming**: Must verify that uploading a 500MB file uses < 50MB of RAM by leveraging PHP streams.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Extends storage capabilities without breaking the Core interface.\n\n=== HUB-12 ===\n# PHASE HUB-12: Notification Service\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Notify\n\n## Description\nA unified multi-channel notification engine. It provides a standardized way to send messages to users via Email, In-app notifications, Webhooks, and SMS. It handles template rendering, queuing, and delivery tracking.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `HUB-04: Identity`, `HUB-10: Queue`, `CORE-12: Compiler`.\n- **Channels**: SMTP/API for Email, Database/Redis for In-app, HTTP for Webhooks.\n- **Templating**: Uses SuperPHP (`CORE-12`) for rendering email and message templates.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **NotificationManager**: Routes notifications to the appropriate channels.\n- **ChannelInterface**: Defines the contract for delivery mechanisms.\n- **Notification**: A class defining the content for each channel (e.g., `toMail`, `toDatabase`).\n- **WebhookDispatcher**: Specialized channel for outbound system events to external URLs.\n\n### Notification Example\n```php\nclass OrderShipped extends Notification\n{\n    public function via($notifiable) { return ['mail', 'database']; }\n\n    public function toMail($notifiable)\n    {\n        return (new MailMessage)\n            ->subject('Order Shipped')\n            ->view('emails.shipped', ['order' => $this->order]);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### NotifierInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface NotifierInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Send a notification to a specific user or collection of users.\n     */\n    public function send(mixed $notifiables, object $notification): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Send a notification immediately (bypassing the queue).\n     */\n    public function sendNow(mixed $notifiables, object $notification): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Depends on `HUB-10` for background delivery and `HUB-04` for user contact details.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications trigger notifications by calling `send()` on the Hub service.\n- **UI**: Hub provides a standard SuperPHP component for displaying in-app notification toasts (`s:ui:notifications`).\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Channel Fallback**: If an email fails to send, it must be logged and marked as \"failed\" without crashing the job worker.\n- **Rate Limiting**: Must verify that no more than 10 webhooks are dispatched to a single endpoint per second (referencing HUB-07).\n- **Template Rendering**: Must verify that the SuperPHP compiler correctly hydrates email templates with dynamic data.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Standardizes user communication across the stack.\n\n=== HUB-13 ===\n# PHASE HUB-13: I18n & L10n Service\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Translator\n\n## Description\nA comprehensive internationalization (i18n) and localization (l10n) service. It provides translation management, number/date formatting, and pluralization support. It centralizes language files at the Hub level while allowing Spokes to define their own local overrides.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-10: Config`, `HUB-02: Cache`.\n- implementation focused on fast array-based lookups and PHP 8.3 features.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **Translator**: The main service for retrieving translated strings (`trans()`).\n- **Loader**: Loads translation files (PHP arrays or JSON) from multiple directories (Hub + Spoke).\n- **Formatter**: Handles dynamic replacement of placeholders and locale-aware number/date formatting.\n- **Pluralizer**: Implements rule-based pluralization for different languages (e.g., Arabic has 6 plural forms).\n\n### Translation File Example\n```php\n// resources/lang/en/messages.php\nreturn [\n    'welcome' => 'Welcome, :name!',\n    'items' => '{0} No items|{1} One item|[2,*] :count items',\n];\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### TranslatorInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface TranslatorInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Translate the given message.\n     */\n    public function get(string $key, array $replace = [], ?string $locale = null): string;\n\n    /**\n     * Get the current application locale.\n     */\n    public function getLocale(): string;\n\n    /**\n     * Set the current application locale.\n     */\n    public function setLocale(string $locale): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Integrated into the `CORE-18` Kernel to detect locale from request headers or session (`HUB-04`).\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications use the `trans()` helper or `@lang` SuperPHP directive.\n- **Persistence**: Locales are stored in the user's session or a cookie.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Fallback Logic**: If a key is missing in `fr-CA`, it must fall back to `fr`, then to the default locale (e.g., `en`).\n- **Performance**: Retrieving a simple string from a hot cache must take < 0.01ms.\n- **UTF-8 Integrity**: Must verify correct rendering of complex scripts (Arabic, CJK, Emojis).\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Enables global availability of the stack.\n\n=== HUB-14 ===\n# PHASE HUB-14: Search Abstraction Layer\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Search\n\n## Description\nA unified abstraction for full-text search. It provides a common API for indexing and querying data, supporting multiple backends like Database (LIKE/Fulltext), Meilisearch, or Elasticsearch. It allows Spoke applications to implement advanced search features without coupling to a specific engine.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-19: DBAL`, `HUB-10: Queue`.\n- **Drivers**: Database (default), Meilisearch (recommended for speed), Algolia (cloud).\n- **Patterns**: Indexer, Searcher, Result Transformer.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **SearchManager**: Factory for resolving I -->|Queue| Q[Search Engine]\n    U[User Query] -->|Search| Q\n    Q -->|Results| R[Result Collection]\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### SearchInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface SearchInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Search an index for the given query.\n     */\n    public function search(string $index, string $query): SearchBuilder;\n\n    /**\n     * Add or update records in the search index.\n     */\n    public function update(string $index, array $records): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Remove records from the search index.\n     */\n    public function delete(string $index, array $ids): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Uses `HUB-10` for asynchronous indexing.\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications implement `SearchableInterface` on their domain entities.\n- **UI**: Hub provides a \"Global Search\" API via `HUB-08` (Gateway).\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Consistency**: A record updated in the DB must appear in the search index within 5 seconds (asynchronous lag check).\n- **Driver Agnostic**: The same search query must return comparable results on both Database and Meilisearch drivers.\n- **Error Resilience**: If the search engine is down, the system must fall back to a database search or return an empty result without crashing.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Adds advanced discovery capabilities to the stack.\n\n=== HUB-15 ===\n# PHASE HUB-15: Health Check & Service Discovery\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Pulse (Health)\n\n## Description\nA monitoring and service discovery registry. It provides a centralized dashboard and API for verifying the health of all Hub services and Spoke applications. It checks database connectivity, disk space, external API availability, and memory usage.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-10: Config`, `CORE-14: Filesystem`, `HUB-02: Cache`.\n- **Standards**: Health Check Response Format (draft-in-ietf-appsawg-service-health-check).\n- **Patterns**: Registry, Pulse/Heartbeat.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **HealthManager**: Orchestrates health checks across the stack.\n- **CheckInterface**: Contract for individual diagnostic tasks (e.g., `DatabaseCheck`, `RedisCheck`).\n- **ServiceRegistry**: A directory of active Hub/Spoke endpoints and their current status.\n- **PulseEndpoint**: A secure `/health` route that returns the system status in JSON.\n\n### Health Check Example\n```php\nclass DatabaseCheck implements CheckInterface\n{\n    public function check(): HealthResult\n    {\n        try {\n            DB::connection()->getPdo();\n            return HealthResult::ok('Connected');\n        } catch (\\Exception $e) {\n            return HealthResult::fail('Disconnected: ' . $e->getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### HealthRegistryInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface HealthRegistryInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Register a new health check.\n     */\n    public function register(string $name, CheckInterface $check): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Run all registered checks and return the status.\n     */\n    public function status(): array;\n\n    /**\n     * Report the status of a specific service.\n     */\n    public function heartbeat(string $service, string $status): void;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Built on the `CORE-13` CLI and `CORE-18` Kernel.\n- **Downward**: Every Spoke application reports its health to the Hub registry via a scheduled job (`HUB-10`).\n- **Monitoring**: Integrates with external tools (e.g., Render metrics) by providing a standardized health endpoint.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Failing Fast**: A critical failure (e.g., DB down) must return a `503 Service Unavailable` status on the health endpoint.\n- **Overhead**: A health check execution must not consume more than 5% of CPU or take longer than 500ms.\n- **Reporting Accuracy**: The registry must correctly identify \"Stale\" services if a heartbeat hasn't been received for 5 minutes.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Essential for production observability and reliability.\n\n=== HUB-16 ===\n# PHASE HUB-16: Hub-level Orchestration Hooks\n\n## Tier\nHub\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Hub Weaver\n\n## Description\nThe \"Weaver\" provides the integration logic for Hub-tier repositories to report their status back to the `CORE-01` Polyrepo Orchestrator. It automates the dependency validation between Hub and Core tiers and prepares the Hub for Spoke consumption.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Depends on**: `CORE-01: Polyrepo Orchestrator`, `HUB-15: Health Check`.\n- **Patterns**: Hook/Callback, CI Integration.\n- **Automation**: Uses `CORE-01` API to register Hub-tier releases and trigger downstream Spoke builds.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **OrchestrationClient**: Communicates with the \"Loom\" (`CORE-01`) via secure webhooks or CLI calls.\n- **DependencyVerifier**: Ensures that the current Hub version is compatible with the installed Core tier version.\n- **ReleaseManager**: Handles tagging and manifest generation for Hub-tier distribution.\n- **SpokeNotifier**: Triggers CI pipelines in Spoke repositories when a Hub update is published.\n\n### Orchestration Flow\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant H as Hub Repo\n    participant W as Weaver Hook\n    participant L as Loom (CORE-01)\n    participant S as Spoke Repos\n    H->>W: commit/push\n    W->>L: reportStatus(CI_PASS)\n    L->>L: validateCoreCompatibility()\n    L->>L: createHubRelease()\n    L->>S: triggerDownstreamBuilds()\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### OrchestratorHookInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface OrchestratorHookInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Notify the Orchestrator of a successful build.\n     */\n    public function notifyBuildSuccess(string $repo, string $commit): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Verify compatibility with the Core tier.\n     */\n    public function checkCoreCompatibility(string $requiredVersion): bool;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Directly integrates with the API defined in `CORE-01`.\n- **Downward**: This is the \"Merge Gate\" for the Hub tier. No Hub component can be considered \"Stable\" until the Weaver has verified it.\n- **CLI**: Provides `s-cli hub:release` to automate the entire Hub-to-Orchestrator handshake.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Version Gating**: Must block a Hub release if it depends on a Core version that is not yet tagged as Stable.\n- **Notification Reliability**: Orchestrator notifications must implement a retry policy (up to 3 times) in case of network failure.\n- **Manifest Accuracy**: The generated `hub-manifest.json` must include every Hub service and its verified version.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Major**. Completes the automated polyrepo lifecycle for the Hub tier.\n\n=== HUB-17 ===\n# PHASE HUB-17: Webhook Ingestion & Dispatch Engine\n\n## Tier\nHub (Shared Services)\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Webhook Nexus\n\n## Description\nA centralized engine for receiving incoming webhooks from external services (e.g., Stripe, GitHub, Shopify) and dispatching them to internal Hub services or Spoke handlers. It provides signature verification, idempotent processing, retry logic, and an audit trail for all webhook activity.\n\n## Sequencing Rationale\nDepends on the Event Bus (HUB-09) and Queue System (HUB-10) to handle asynchronous processing and the Audit Log (HUB-06) for tracking. It precedes Spoke applications that rely on external event triggers.\n\n## Context7 Research\n- **Direct Hub Dependencies**: `HUB-09: Event Bus`, `HUB-10: Queue & Job Dispatcher`, `HUB-06: Audit Log`, `HUB-08: API Gateway`.\n- **Transitive Core Dependencies**: `CORE-06: Router`, `CORE-04: HTTP Message`, `CORE-19: DBAL`, `CORE-03: Event Dispatcher`.\n- **Security Patterns**: Hmac-based signature verification (SHA-256), IP whitelisting.\n- **Resiliency**: Dead Letter Queues (DLQ) for failed dispatches.\n\n## Architectural Design\n- **WebhookIngestor**: Entry point for all incoming HTTP POST requests.\n- **SignatureValidator**: Extensible validator for different provider signature formats.\n- **DispatchRegistry**: Maps incoming webhook types to internal Hub events or Spoke-specific jobs.\n- **IdempotencyManager**: Prevents duplicate processing using a persistent cache of request IDs.\n\n### Webhook Dispatch Flow\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Ext as External Service\n    participant GW as Gateway (HUB-08)\n    participant W as Webhook Nexus\n    participant Q as Queue (HUB-10)\n    participant B as Event Bus (HUB-09)\n    Ext->>GW: POST /webhooks/provider\n    GW->>W: process(Request)\n    W->>W: verifySignature()\n    W->>W: checkIdempotency()\n    W->>Q: push(WebhookJob)\n    Q->>B: emit(WebhookEvent)\n    W-->>Ext: 200 OK\n```\n\n## Interface Contracts\n\n### WebhookManagerInterface\n```php\nnamespace Sovereign\\Hub\\Contracts;\n\ninterface WebhookManagerInterface\n{\n    /**\n     * Register a webhook handler for a specific provider/event.\n     */\n    public function subscribe(string $provider, string $event, callable $handler): void;\n\n    /**\n     * Verify the signature of an incoming request.\n     */\n    public function verify(string $provider, string $payload, array $headers): bool;\n}\n```\n\n## Integration Strategy\n- **Upward**: Registered as a route within `HUB-08` (API Gateway).\n- **Downward**: Spoke applications register \"Webhook Listeners\" that hook into the `HUB-09` Event Bus.\n- **Contract**: Dispatches standardized `WebhookReceivedEvent` objects containing the verified payload and provider metadata.\n\n## CI Verification Criteria\n- **Signature Security**: Must reject requests with invalid signatures for at least 3 major providers (Stripe, GitHub, Generic).\n- **Idempotency**: Processing the same request ID twice must result in exactly one side effect.\n- **Auditability**: Every received webhook must have an entry in the `webhook_logs` table with status and processing time.\n\n## SemVer Impact\n**Minor**. Adds webhook handling capabilities to the Hub.\n\n=== HUB-18 ===\n# PHASE HUB-18: Media Processing Coordination Service\n\n## Tier\nHub (Shared Services)\n\n## Component Name\nSovereign Media Forge\n\n## Description\nA coordinated service for handling media assets (images, video, documents).
